// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
// FORENSIC AUDIT â€” Path Counters
// Track which audio processing path is actually being used at runtime.
// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
namespace PitchAuditCore {
    inline std::atomic<int> normalPathHits{0};
    inline std::atomic<int> independentPitchPathHits{0};
    inline std::atomic<int> stretchOnlyPathHits{0};
    inline std::atomic<int> oldTimePitchDSPHits{0};
    inline std::atomic<int> resampleTapeHits{0};
}

namespace DAW {

static inline float applyClipFadeCurve(float x) noexcept
{
    x = juce::jlimit(0.0f, 1.0f, x);
    return x;
}

/**
 * AudioEngine â€” real-time audio processor.
 *
 * Processes nodes in routing-graph topological order:
 *   Track nodes â†’ Bus nodes â†’ Master node â†’ hardware output.
 *
 * Per-node: source rendering, plugin inserts, volume, pan, mute/solo,
 *           send taps (pre/post), peak metering.
 *
 * All audio MUST pass through the Master node before reaching hardware.
 * NO track or bus may bypass Master in default routing.
 *
 * Thread safety: process() runs on the audio thread.
 * All shared state uses atomics or lock-free reads.
 */
class AudioEngine : public juce::Timer
{
public:
    AudioEngine() = default;

    void setSubsystems(TrackManager* tracks, ClipManager* clips,
                       TransportController* transport, RoutingGraph* routing,
                       AudioFileManager* audioFiles = nullptr)
    {
        tracks_     = tracks;
        clips_      = clips;
        transport_  = transport;
        routing_    = routing;
        audioFiles_ = audioFiles;
    }

    /** Supply the per-track plugin chains (owned by ApplicationCore). */
    void setPluginChains(std::map<TrackID, std::unique_ptr<PluginChainCore>>* chains)
    {
        pluginChains_ = chains;
    }

    /** Supply MIDI playback core for MIDI tracks. */
    void setMidiPlayback(DAW::PianoRollPlaybackCore* pb) noexcept { midiPlayback_ = pb; }
    void setMidiInput(DAW::MidiInputCore* input) noexcept { midiInput_ = input; }
    void setVirtualKeyboard(DAW::VirtualMidiKeyboardCore* kb) noexcept { virtualKeyboard_ = kb; }

    /** Supply the FolderBus state model so the audio thread reads pre-baked mute/solo. */
    void setFolderBusStateModel(FolderBusStateModel* model) { folderBusStateModel_ = model; }

    void prepare(double sampleRate, int blockSize)
    {
        sampleRate_ = sampleRate;
        blockSize_  = blockSize;
        mixBuffer_.setSize(2, blockSize);
        trackBuffer_.setSize(2, blockSize);
        preFxBuffer_.setSize(2, blockSize);
        scratchBuf_.assign(blockSize * 8, 0.f); // 8x for stretch overrun

        // Per-clip independent pitch cores are created on demand in getOrCreateClipPitchCore()
        deepDemonDSP_.prepare(sampleRate, blockSize);
        demonVoiceEngine_.prepare(sampleRate, blockSize, 2);
        pitchInputBuffer_.setSize(2, blockSize);
        pitchOutputBuffer_.setSize(2, blockSize);

        tapeScratchBuf_.setSize(2, blockSize, false, true, true);
        rebuildPdcLines(blockSize);

        // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
        // FORENSIC AUDIT â€” Start Counter Report Timer
        // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
        startTimer(2000); // Report every 2 seconds
    }

    void releaseResources()
    {
        mixBuffer_.setSize(0, 0);
        trackBuffer_.setSize(0, 0);
        nodeBuffers_.clear();
        clipDspMap_.clear();
        lastClipRenderEndOffset_.clear();
        lastClipRenderBlock_.clear();
        lastClipRenderMode_.clear();
    }

    /** Called on the audio thread. Fills the output buffer. */
    void process(const juce::AudioSourceChannelInfo& output)
    {
        struct PlaybackPitchWriteScope
        {
            PlaybackPitchWriteScope() noexcept
            {
                DAW::PitchWriteAudit::playbackActive.store(true, std::memory_order_relaxed);
            }

            ~PlaybackPitchWriteScope() noexcept
            {
                DAW::PitchWriteAudit::playbackActive.store(false, std::memory_order_relaxed);
            }
        } playbackPitchWriteScope;

        output.clearActiveBufferRegion();

        if (!tracks_ || !transport_) return;

        bool isPlaying = transport_->isPlaying();
        auto position  = transport_->getPosition();
        int  numSamples = output.numSamples;
        ++processCounter_;

        const bool transportDiscontinuous = (!lastWasPlaying_ && isPlaying)
            || (lastWasPlaying_ && !isPlaying)
            || (isPlaying && lastWasPlaying_
                && (position < lastTransportPos_
                    || std::abs((double)(position - lastTransportPos_ - lastProcessSamples_)) > 1.0));

        if (transportDiscontinuous)
            resetAllClipDSPState();

        // â”€â”€ Read FolderBus snapshot (mute/solo, built on message thread) â”€â”€â”€â”€
        std::shared_ptr<const FolderBusSnapshot> fbSnap;
        if (folderBusStateModel_)
            fbSnap = folderBusStateModel_->get();

        // Legacy flat solo detection (used when no FolderBus snapshot available)
        bool anySolo = false;
        if (!fbSnap || fbSnap->effectiveSoloSet.empty())
            for (int i = 0; i < tracks_->getNumTracks(); ++i)
                if (tracks_->getTrack(i)->isSoloed()) { anySolo = true; break; }

        // â”€â”€ Route-aware processing â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
        // If routing graph is available, process in topological order
        // so buses receive their inputs before being processed.
        //
        // THREAD SAFETY: graphLock_ guards nodes_ / connections_ in RoutingGraph.
        // The audio thread uses ScopedTryLock â€” if the message thread is mutating
        // the graph (addNode / removeNode / connect / disconnect) we skip this block
        // and output silence rather than racing on the containers.  One silent block
        // (~10 ms) is far preferable to a crash.
        if (routing_)
        {
            juce::ScopedTryLock sl(routing_->getLock());
            if (sl.isLocked())
            {
                processWithRouting(output, numSamples, isPlaying, position, anySolo, fbSnap.get());
            }
            else
            {
                // Graph is being mutated â€” output silence this block, no crash.
                output.clearActiveBufferRegion();
                DBG("[AudioEngine] Skipped block: routing graph locked by message thread");
            }
        }
        else
        {
            // Fallback: simple flat mix (no routing graph)
            processFlat(output, numSamples, isPlaying, position, anySolo);
        }

        // â”€â”€ Advance transport â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
        if (isPlaying)
        {
            auto newPos = position + numSamples;

            if (transport_->isLooping())
            {
                auto [ls, le] = transport_->getLoopRange();
                if (le > ls && newPos >= le)
                    newPos = ls + (newPos - le) % juce::jmax((SamplePosition)1, le - ls);
            }

            transport_->setPositionFromAudioThread(newPos);
        }

        lastWasPlaying_    = isPlaying;
        lastTransportPos_  = position;
        lastProcessSamples_ = numSamples;

        // â”€â”€ Master peak metering (for legacy compatibility) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
        float masterPeakL = output.buffer->getMagnitude(0, output.startSample, numSamples);
        float masterPeakR = output.buffer->getMagnitude(
            juce::jmin(1, output.buffer->getNumChannels() - 1), output.startSample, numSamples);
        masterPeakLevel_.store(juce::jmax(masterPeakL, masterPeakR), std::memory_order_relaxed);

        // Feed master track meter so the MixerStrip sees real stereo levels
        if (tracks_->hasMasterTrack())
        {
            auto* master = tracks_->getMasterTrack();
            float dispL = juce::jmax(masterPeakL, master->getPeakLevelLeft()  * 0.92f);
            float dispR = juce::jmax(masterPeakR, master->getPeakLevelRight() * 0.92f);
            master->setPeakLevels(dispL, dispR);
        }

    }

    float getMasterPeakLevel() const { return masterPeakLevel_.load(std::memory_order_relaxed); }

    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
    // FORENSIC AUDIT â€” Timer Callback for Path Counter Report
    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
    void timerCallback() override
    {
        FORENSIC_LOG("â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•");
        FORENSIC_LOG("[FORENSIC AUDIT] Path Counter Report (every 2 seconds)");
        FORENSIC_LOG("â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•");
        FORENSIC_LOG("  normalPathHits:           " << juce::String(PitchAuditCore::normalPathHits.load()));
        FORENSIC_LOG("  independentPitchPathHits: " << juce::String(PitchAuditCore::independentPitchPathHits.load()));
        FORENSIC_LOG("  stretchOnlyPathHits:      " << juce::String(PitchAuditCore::stretchOnlyPathHits.load()));
        FORENSIC_LOG("  oldTimePitchDSPHits:      " << juce::String(PitchAuditCore::oldTimePitchDSPHits.load()));
        FORENSIC_LOG("  resampleTapeHits:         " << juce::String(PitchAuditCore::resampleTapeHits.load()));
        FORENSIC_LOG("â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•");

        // If independentPitchPathHits == 0 while moving pitch knob â†’ SUSPECT 1 CONFIRMED
        if (PitchAuditCore::independentPitchPathHits.load() == 0 &&
            PitchAuditCore::oldTimePitchDSPHits.load() > 0)
        {
            FORENSIC_LOG("âš ï¸âš ï¸âš ï¸ SUSPECT 1 CONFIRMED âš ï¸âš ï¸âš ï¸");
            FORENSIC_LOG("Independent pitch path is NEVER executed!");
            FORENSIC_LOG("Old TimePitchDSPCore is being used instead.");
        }
    }

private:
    TrackManager*        tracks_     = nullptr;
    ClipManager*         clips_      = nullptr;
    TransportController* transport_  = nullptr;
    RoutingGraph*        routing_    = nullptr;
    AudioFileManager*    audioFiles_ = nullptr;
    std::map<TrackID, std::unique_ptr<PluginChainCore>>* pluginChains_ = nullptr;
    FolderBusStateModel* folderBusStateModel_ = nullptr;
    DAW::PianoRollPlaybackCore* midiPlayback_ = nullptr;
    DAW::MidiInputCore* midiInput_ = nullptr;
    DAW::VirtualMidiKeyboardCore* virtualKeyboard_ = nullptr;

    double sampleRate_ = 44100.0;
    int    blockSize_  = 512;

    juce::AudioBuffer<float> mixBuffer_;
    juce::AudioBuffer<float> trackBuffer_;
    std::atomic<float> masterPeakLevel_{0.f};

    // Per-clip DSP cores for Mode 1-6 processing (one per unique ClipID)
    std::map<ClipID, std::unique_ptr<ArrangementEditor::TimePitchDSPCore>> clipDspMap_;
    std::map<ClipID, SamplePosition> lastClipRenderEndOffset_;
    std::map<ClipID, uint64_t>       lastClipRenderBlock_;
    std::map<ClipID, int>            lastClipRenderMode_;
    uint64_t                         processCounter_ = 0;
    SamplePosition                   lastTransportPos_ = 0;
    int                              lastProcessSamples_ = 0;
    bool                             lastWasPlaying_ = false;

    // Scratch buffer for renderClipSegment output before gain/fade mix
    std::vector<float> scratchBuf_;

    // Independent pitch cores â€” one per clip (preserves grain state per clip)
    std::map<ClipID, std::unique_ptr<ArrangementEditor::ClipIndependentPitchCore>> clipPitchCoreMap_;
    std::map<ClipID, std::unique_ptr<ArrangementEditor::PitchSmootherCore>> clipPitchSmootherMap_;

    // Demon-voice post-processing cores â€” one per clip, lazily created.
    std::map<ClipID, std::unique_ptr<ArrangementEditor::FormantShiftCore>>           clipFormantMap_;
    std::map<ClipID, std::unique_ptr<ArrangementEditor::DarkVoiceBodyCore>>           clipBodyMap_;
    std::map<ClipID, std::unique_ptr<ArrangementEditor::TapeResamplePitchEngineCore>> clipTapeMap_;
    // Scratch tape buffer â€” reused every block, pre-sized in prepare().
    juce::AudioBuffer<float> tapeScratchBuf_;
    ArrangementEditor::VoiceTransformDSPCore deepDemonDSP_;
    ArrangementEditor::DemonVoiceEngineCore demonVoiceEngine_;
    juce::AudioBuffer<float> pitchInputBuffer_;
    juce::AudioBuffer<float> pitchOutputBuffer_;

    // Per-node audio buffers for routing-graph-aware processing
    std::map<juce::String, juce::AudioBuffer<float>> nodeBuffers_;

    // Per-node sidechain accumulator buffers (keyed by destination node id)
    std::map<juce::String, juce::AudioBuffer<float>> sidechainBuffers_;

    // Pre-FX tap: snapshot of the track buffer captured BEFORE the plugin chain runs
    juce::AudioBuffer<float> preFxBuffer_;

    // PDC delay lines for sidechain edges:
    //   key   = destNodeId
    //   value = circular delay buffer [2 ch x maxDelaySamples]
    // Each sidechain destination may need compensation when its source track
    // has lookahead plugins that introduce latency.
    struct PdcDelayLine
    {
        juce::AudioBuffer<float> buf;   // [2 x capacity]
        int writePos = 0;
        int delaySamples = 0;

        void prepare(int capacity)
        {
            buf.setSize(2, juce::jmax(1, capacity));
            buf.clear();
            writePos = 0;
        }

        void push(const float* L, const float* R, int n)
        {
            const int cap = buf.getNumSamples();
            auto* wL = buf.getWritePointer(0);
            auto* wR = buf.getWritePointer(1);
            for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i)
            {
                wL[writePos] = L[i];
                wR[writePos] = R[i];
                writePos = (writePos + 1) % cap;
            }
        }

        // Read `n` samples delayed by `delaySamples` from the current write head
        void read(float* outL, float* outR, int n) const
        {
            const int cap = buf.getNumSamples();
            const auto* rL = buf.getReadPointer(0);
            const auto* rR = buf.getReadPointer(1);
            for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i)
            {
                int pos = (writePos - delaySamples - n + i + cap * 4) % cap;
                outL[i] = rL[pos];
                outR[i] = rR[pos];
            }
        }
    };
    std::map<juce::String, PdcDelayLine> pdcLines_;

    /** Returns (creating if necessary) the per-clip ClipIndependentPitchCore. */
    ArrangementEditor::ClipIndependentPitchCore& getOrCreateClipPitchCore(const ClipID& id)
    {
        auto it = clipPitchCoreMap_.find(id);
        if (it == clipPitchCoreMap_.end())
        {
            auto core = std::make_unique<ArrangementEditor::ClipIndependentPitchCore>();
            core->prepare(sampleRate_, blockSize_, 2);
            it = clipPitchCoreMap_.emplace(id, std::move(core)).first;
        }
        return *it->second;
    }

    /** Returns (creating if necessary) the per-clip pitch smoother used by the connected audio path. */
    ArrangementEditor::PitchSmootherCore& getOrCreateClipPitchSmoother(const ClipID& id)
    {
        auto it = clipPitchSmootherMap_.find(id);
        if (it == clipPitchSmootherMap_.end())
        {
            auto smoother = std::make_unique<ArrangementEditor::PitchSmootherCore>();
            smoother->prepare(sampleRate_, 0.050);
            it = clipPitchSmootherMap_.emplace(id, std::move(smoother)).first;
        }
        return *it->second;
    }

    ArrangementEditor::FormantShiftCore& getOrCreateFormantCore(const ClipID& id)
    {
        auto it = clipFormantMap_.find(id);
        if (it == clipFormantMap_.end())
        {
            auto core = std::make_unique<ArrangementEditor::FormantShiftCore>();
            core->prepare(sampleRate_, blockSize_);
            it = clipFormantMap_.emplace(id, std::move(core)).first;
        }
        return *it->second;
    }

    ArrangementEditor::DarkVoiceBodyCore& getOrCreateBodyCore(const ClipID& id)
    {
        auto it = clipBodyMap_.find(id);
        if (it == clipBodyMap_.end())
        {
            auto core = std::make_unique<ArrangementEditor::DarkVoiceBodyCore>();
            core->prepare(sampleRate_, blockSize_);
            it = clipBodyMap_.emplace(id, std::move(core)).first;
        }
        return *it->second;
    }

    ArrangementEditor::TapeResamplePitchEngineCore& getOrCreateTapeCore(const ClipID& id)
    {
        auto it = clipTapeMap_.find(id);
        if (it == clipTapeMap_.end())
        {
            auto core = std::make_unique<ArrangementEditor::TapeResamplePitchEngineCore>();
            core->prepare(sampleRate_, blockSize_);
            it = clipTapeMap_.emplace(id, std::move(core)).first;
        }
        return *it->second;
    }

    /** Returns (creating if necessary) the per-clip TimePitchDSPCore. */
    ArrangementEditor::TimePitchDSPCore& getOrCreateClipDSP(const ClipID& id)
    {
        auto it = clipDspMap_.find(id);
        if (it == clipDspMap_.end())
        {
            auto core = std::make_unique<ArrangementEditor::TimePitchDSPCore>();
            core->prepare(sampleRate_, blockSize_);
            it = clipDspMap_.emplace(id, std::move(core)).first;
        }
        return *it->second;
    }

    void resetAllClipDSPState()
    {
        for (auto& [id, dsp] : clipDspMap_)
            if (dsp)
                dsp->reset();

        for (auto& [id, core] : clipPitchCoreMap_)
            if (core)
                core->reset();

        for (auto& [id, smoother] : clipPitchSmootherMap_)
            if (smoother)
                smoother->reset(0.0f);

        for (auto& [id, core] : clipFormantMap_)
            if (core) core->reset();
        for (auto& [id, core] : clipBodyMap_)
            if (core) core->reset();
        for (auto& [id, core] : clipTapeMap_)
            if (core) core->reset();

        lastClipRenderEndOffset_.clear();
        lastClipRenderBlock_.clear();
        lastClipRenderMode_.clear();
    }

    /** Inline fade gain helper shared between Mode 0 and Mode 1-6 paths. */
    float computeFadeGain(int posInClip, int clipLength,
                          int fadeInLen, int fadeOutLen) const noexcept
    {
        float g = 1.0f;
        if (fadeInLen > 0 && posInClip < fadeInLen)
            g *= applyClipFadeCurve((float)posInClip / (float)juce::jmax(1, fadeInLen));
        const int fadeOutStart = juce::jmax(0, clipLength - fadeOutLen);
        if (fadeOutLen > 0 && posInClip >= fadeOutStart)
        {
            const float x = (float)(posInClip - fadeOutStart) / (float)juce::jmax(1, fadeOutLen);
            g *= applyClipFadeCurve(juce::jlimit(0.f, 1.f, 1.f - x));
        }
        return g;
    }

    /** (Re)build PDC delay lines based on current plugin chain latencies.
     *  Call after prepare() and whenever a plugin is added/removed.
     *  For each sidechain dest node, computes how many samples the source track
     *  chain is ahead of the dest track's main path and sets up a matching delay. */
    void rebuildPdcLines(int numSamples)
    {
        if (!routing_ || !pluginChains_) return;
        for (auto* conn : routing_->getAllConnections())
        {
            if (!conn || conn->type != ConnectionType::Sidechain) continue;
            auto* srcNode  = routing_->getNode(conn->sourceNodeId);
            auto* destNode = routing_->getNode(conn->destNodeId);
            if (!srcNode || !destNode) continue;

            // Source chain latency (lookahead)
            int srcLatency = 0;
            auto srcIt = pluginChains_->find(srcNode->trackId);
            if (srcIt != pluginChains_->end() && srcIt->second)
                srcLatency = srcIt->second->totalLatencySamples();

            // Dest chain latency (its own plugins before sidechain processing)
            int destLatency = 0;
            auto destIt = pluginChains_->find(destNode->trackId);
            if (destIt != pluginChains_->end() && destIt->second)
                destLatency = destIt->second->totalLatencySamples();

            // How many extra samples the sidechain signal needs to be delayed
            // to stay aligned with the dest track's audio path
            int delaySamples = juce::jmax(0, srcLatency - destLatency);

            auto& line = pdcLines_[conn->destNodeId];
            int capacity = juce::jmax(numSamples * 4, delaySamples + numSamples * 2);
            if (line.buf.getNumSamples() < capacity)
                line.prepare(capacity);
            line.delaySamples = delaySamples;
        }
    }

    // â”€â”€ Routing-graph-aware processing â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    void processWithRouting(const juce::AudioSourceChannelInfo& output,
                            int numSamples, bool isPlaying,
                            SamplePosition position, bool anySolo,
                            const FolderBusSnapshot* fbSnap)
    {
        auto processingOrder = routing_->getProcessingOrder();

        // Ensure per-node buffers exist and are cleared
        for (const auto& nodeId : processingOrder)
        {
            auto& buf = nodeBuffers_[nodeId];
            if (buf.getNumSamples() < numSamples || buf.getNumChannels() < 2)
                buf.setSize(2, juce::jmax(numSamples, blockSize_));
            buf.clear(0, numSamples);

            // Sidechain accumulator for this node
            auto& sc = sidechainBuffers_[nodeId];
            if (sc.getNumSamples() < numSamples || sc.getNumChannels() < 2)
                sc.setSize(2, juce::jmax(numSamples, blockSize_));
            sc.clear(0, numSamples);
        }

        // Process each node in topological order
        for (const auto& nodeId : processingOrder)
        {
            auto* node = routing_->getNode(nodeId);
            if (!node || !node->active) continue;

            switch (node->type)
            {
                case RoutingNodeType::Track:
                    processTrackNode(node, numSamples, isPlaying, position, anySolo, fbSnap);
                    break;

                case RoutingNodeType::Bus:
                case RoutingNodeType::FolderBus:
                    processBusNode(node, numSamples, anySolo, fbSnap);
                    break;

                case RoutingNodeType::Master:
                    processMasterNode(node, numSamples, output);
                    break;

                case RoutingNodeType::Hardware:
                    break; // handled externally
            }
        }
    }

    /** Process a Track node: render clips, apply plugins/vol/pan, route to outputs. */
    void processTrackNode(RoutingNode* node, int numSamples,
                          bool isPlaying, SamplePosition position, bool anySolo,
                          const FolderBusSnapshot* fbSnap)
    {
        auto* track = tracks_->getTrack(node->trackId);
        if (!track) return;

        auto& nodeBuf = nodeBuffers_[node->id];

        // Render clips into node buffer
        // THREAD SAFETY: clips_ OwnedArray is protected by ClipManager::clipLock_.
        // Hold a ScopedTryLock for the entire getClipsOnTrack + renderClip loop so
        // no Clip* can be deleted mid-iteration on the message thread.
        // If the lock can't be obtained this block produces silence for this track (rare, acceptable).
        trackBuffer_.clear(0, numSamples);
        if (clips_)
        {
            juce::ScopedTryLock cl(clips_->getLock());
            if (cl.isLocked())
            {
                auto clipsOnTrack = clips_->getClipsOnTrack(track->getID());
                for (auto* clip : clipsOnTrack)
                {
                    if (clip->isMuted()) continue;
                    if (isPlaying)
                        renderClip(clip, position, numSamples);
                }
            }
            else
            {
                DBG("[AudioEngine] Skipped clip render for track " + track->getID()
                    + ": clip manager locked by message thread");
            }
        }

        // Mix in any incoming send contributions accumulated into this node's buffer.
        // Upstream tracks that send to this track wrote into nodeBuffers_[node->id]
        // earlier in the topological processing pass. Add them to trackBuffer_ now
        // so they pass through this track's plugin chain, fader, and pan.
        {
            auto& incomingBuf = nodeBuffers_[node->id];
            auto* srcL = incomingBuf.getReadPointer(0);
            auto* srcR = incomingBuf.getReadPointer(juce::jmin(1, incomingBuf.getNumChannels() - 1));
            auto* dstL = trackBuffer_.getWritePointer(0);
            auto* dstR = trackBuffer_.getWritePointer(juce::jmin(1, trackBuffer_.getNumChannels() - 1));
            for (int s = 0; s < numSamples; ++s)
            {
                dstL[s] += srcL[s];
                dstR[s] += srcR[s];
            }
        }

        // Capture pre-FX snapshot before the plugin chain runs (for PreFX tap)
        if (preFxBuffer_.getNumSamples() < numSamples || preFxBuffer_.getNumChannels() < 2)
            preFxBuffer_.setSize(2, juce::jmax(numSamples, blockSize_));
        for (int ch = 0; ch < 2; ++ch)
            preFxBuffer_.copyFrom(ch, 0, trackBuffer_, ch, 0, numSamples);

        // MIDI playback: generate note events for MIDI tracks and feed to instrument plugin
        juce::MidiBuffer trackMidi;
        if (midiPlayback_ && track->getRole() == TrackRole::MIDI)
        {
            PianoRollPlaybackCore::TransportInfo ti;
            ti.isPlaying          = isPlaying;
            ti.timelinePosSamples = (juce::int64) position;
            ti.numSamples         = numSamples;
            midiPlayback_->processBlock (track->getID(), ti, trackMidi);
        }
        if (midiInput_ && (track->getRole() == TrackRole::MIDI || track->getRole() == TrackRole::Instrument))
            midiInput_->processBlock(track->getID(), trackMidi, numSamples);
        // Virtual keyboard â€” always active for the selected MIDI track
        if (virtualKeyboard_ && track->getRole() == TrackRole::MIDI)
            virtualKeyboard_->processBlock (track->getID(), trackMidi, numSamples);

        // Plugin chain (inserts) â€” pass the sidechain buffer so processors
        // with a sidechain input bus can read it via processBlockWithSidechain().
        if (pluginChains_)
        {
            auto it = pluginChains_->find(track->getID());
            if (it != pluginChains_->end() && it->second)
            {
                auto* node2 = routing_->getNodeByTrackId(track->getID());
                const bool hasMidiInput = track->getRole() == TrackRole::MIDI && !trackMidi.isEmpty();
                bool hasSidechainInput = false;
                if (node2 != nullptr)
                {
                    const auto sidechainInputs = routing_->getSidechainInputs(node2->id);
                    hasSidechainInput = std::any_of(sidechainInputs.begin(),
                                                    sidechainInputs.end(),
                                                    [](RoutingConnection* conn)
                                                    {
                                                        return conn != nullptr && conn->active && !conn->bypassed;
                                                    });
                }

                if (hasMidiInput)
                    it->second->processBlockWithMidi(trackBuffer_, trackMidi, numSamples);

                else if (hasSidechainInput)
                    it->second->processBlockWithSidechain(trackBuffer_,
                                                          sidechainBuffers_[node2->id],
                                                          numSamples);
                else
                    it->second->processBlock(trackBuffer_, numSamples);
            }
        }

        // Volume and pan
        float vol = track->getVolume();
        float pan = track->getPan();

        // Equal-power pan law
        float leftGain  = vol * std::cos((pan + 1.f) * 0.25f * juce::MathConstants<float>::pi);
        float rightGain = vol * std::sin((pan + 1.f) * 0.25f * juce::MathConstants<float>::pi);

        // Peak metering â€” always update for visual display, even when muted
        float leftPeak  = trackBuffer_.getMagnitude(0, 0, numSamples) * leftGain;
        float rightPeak = trackBuffer_.getMagnitude(
            juce::jmin(1, trackBuffer_.getNumChannels() - 1), 0, numSamples) * rightGain;
        float displayLeft = juce::jmax(leftPeak, track->getPeakLevelLeft() * 0.92f);
        float displayRight = juce::jmax(rightPeak, track->getPeakLevelRight() * 0.92f);
        track->setPeakLevels(displayLeft, displayRight);

        // Mute/solo logic â€” snapshot-aware (FolderBus effective sets take precedence)
        bool audible;
        if (fbSnap)
        {
            bool effMuted = fbSnap->effectivelyMutedTracks.count(track->getID()) > 0;
            audible = !effMuted;
            if (!fbSnap->effectiveSoloSet.empty())
                audible = audible && fbSnap->effectiveSoloSet.count(track->getID()) > 0;
        }
        else
        {
            audible = !track->isMuted();
            if (anySolo) audible = track->isSoloed();
        }

        if (!audible) return;

        // â”€â”€ Pre-fader sends â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
        auto outConns = routing_->getOutputConnections(node->id);
        for (auto* conn : outConns)
        {
            if (!conn->active || conn->bypassed) continue;
            if (conn->type != ConnectionType::PreSend) continue;

            auto itBuf = nodeBuffers_.find(conn->destNodeId);
            if (itBuf == nodeBuffers_.end()) continue;

            // Pre-fader send: signal before fader, with send gain
            float sendGain = conn->gain;
            auto& destBuf = itBuf->second;
            auto* srcL = trackBuffer_.getReadPointer(0);
            auto* srcR = trackBuffer_.getReadPointer(juce::jmin(1, trackBuffer_.getNumChannels() - 1));
            auto* dstL = destBuf.getWritePointer(0);
            auto* dstR = destBuf.getWritePointer(juce::jmin(1, destBuf.getNumChannels() - 1));
            for (int s = 0; s < numSamples; ++s)
            {
                dstL[s] += srcL[s] * sendGain;
                dstR[s] += srcR[s] * sendGain;
            }
        }

        // â”€â”€ Apply gain to trackBuffer for post-fader routing â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
        auto* tbL = trackBuffer_.getWritePointer(0);
        auto* tbR = trackBuffer_.getWritePointer(juce::jmin(1, trackBuffer_.getNumChannels() - 1));
        for (int s = 0; s < numSamples; ++s)
        {
            tbL[s] *= leftGain;
            tbR[s] *= rightGain;
        }

        // â”€â”€ Route to outputs (Direct + Post-fader sends) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
        for (auto* conn : outConns)
        {
            if (!conn->active || conn->bypassed) continue;
            if (conn->type == ConnectionType::PreSend || conn->type == ConnectionType::Sidechain)
                continue;

            auto itBuf = nodeBuffers_.find(conn->destNodeId);
            if (itBuf == nodeBuffers_.end()) continue;

            float routeGain = conn->gain;
            auto& destBuf = itBuf->second;
            auto* dstL = destBuf.getWritePointer(0);
            auto* dstR = destBuf.getWritePointer(juce::jmin(1, destBuf.getNumChannels() - 1));
            for (int s = 0; s < numSamples; ++s)
            {
                dstL[s] += tbL[s] * routeGain;
                dstR[s] += tbR[s] * routeGain;
            }
        }

        // â”€â”€ Sidechain sends â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
        // Accumulate source signal into the destination node's sidechain buffer.
        // TapPoint::PreFX  = trackBuffer_ before plugins (raw clips + incoming sends)
        // TapPoint::PostFX = trackBuffer_ after plugins, before fader (already the state here)
        // TapPoint::PostMixer = post-fader (tbL/tbR already has gain applied)
        // We capture the pre-fader state here for PreFX/PostFX use.
        for (auto* conn : outConns)
        {
            if (!conn->active || conn->bypassed) continue;
            if (conn->type != ConnectionType::Sidechain) continue;

            auto itSC = sidechainBuffers_.find(conn->destNodeId);
            if (itSC == sidechainBuffers_.end()) continue;

            float scGain = conn->gain;
            auto& scBuf  = itSC->second;
            auto* scL    = scBuf.getWritePointer(0);
            auto* scR    = scBuf.getWritePointer(juce::jmin(1, scBuf.getNumChannels() - 1));

            // Choose tap point
            const float* tapL = nullptr;
            const float* tapR = nullptr;
            if (conn->tapPoint == TapPoint::PostMixer)
            {
                tapL = tbL;
                tapR = tbR;
            }
            else if (conn->tapPoint == TapPoint::PreFX)
            {
                // True pre-FX: snapshot captured before plugin chain ran
                tapL = preFxBuffer_.getReadPointer(0);
                tapR = preFxBuffer_.getReadPointer(juce::jmin(1, preFxBuffer_.getNumChannels() - 1));
            }
            else // PostFX â€” post-plugin, pre-fader
            {
                tapL = trackBuffer_.getReadPointer(0);
                tapR = trackBuffer_.getReadPointer(juce::jmin(1, trackBuffer_.getNumChannels() - 1));
            }

            // PDC: push into delay line and read compensated output
            auto pdcIt = pdcLines_.find(conn->destNodeId);
            if (pdcIt != pdcLines_.end() && pdcIt->second.delaySamples > 0)
            {
                auto& line = pdcIt->second;
                line.push(tapL, tapR, numSamples);
                // Temp read buffers
                std::vector<float> delayedL(numSamples), delayedR(numSamples);
                line.read(delayedL.data(), delayedR.data(), numSamples);
                for (int s = 0; s < numSamples; ++s)
                {
                    scL[s] += delayedL[s] * scGain;
                    scR[s] += delayedR[s] * scGain;
                }
            }
            else
            {
                for (int s = 0; s < numSamples; ++s)
                {
                    scL[s] += tapL[s] * scGain;
                    scR[s] += tapR[s] * scGain;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /** Process a Bus node: incoming signals already summed, apply bus FX/fader, route out. */
    void processBusNode(RoutingNode* node, int numSamples, bool anySolo,
                        const FolderBusSnapshot* fbSnap)
    {
        auto& nodeBuf = nodeBuffers_[node->id];

        // Look up associated track for bus controls (volume, mute, solo-safe, plugins)
        Track* busTrack = nullptr;
        if (node->trackId.isNotEmpty())
            busTrack = tracks_->getTrack(node->trackId);

        // Bus plugin chain
        if (busTrack && pluginChains_)
        {
            auto it = pluginChains_->find(busTrack->getID());
            if (it != pluginChains_->end() && it->second)
                it->second->processBlock(nodeBuf, numSamples);
        }

        // Bus mute â€” snapshot-aware
        float busGain = busTrack ? busTrack->getVolume() : 1.0f;
        bool  busMuted;
        if (fbSnap && busTrack)
            busMuted = fbSnap->effectivelyMutedTracks.count(busTrack->getID()) > 0;
        else
            busMuted = busTrack ? busTrack->isMuted() : false;

        // Solo logic: a bus is silenced only if it is explicitly muted (or effectively muted)
        if (busMuted) busGain = 0.0f;

        // Bus metering
        if (busTrack)
        {
            float peakL = nodeBuf.getMagnitude(0, 0, numSamples) * busGain;
            float peakR = nodeBuf.getMagnitude(juce::jmin(1, nodeBuf.getNumChannels() - 1), 0, numSamples) * busGain;
            float dispL = juce::jmax(peakL, busTrack->getPeakLevelLeft() * 0.92f);
            float dispR = juce::jmax(peakR, busTrack->getPeakLevelRight() * 0.92f);
            busTrack->setPeakLevels(dispL, dispR);
        }

        if (busGain <= 0.0f) return;

        // Apply bus gain
        auto* bufL = nodeBuf.getWritePointer(0);
        auto* bufR = nodeBuf.getWritePointer(juce::jmin(1, nodeBuf.getNumChannels() - 1));
        if (std::abs(busGain - 1.0f) > 0.0001f)
        {
            for (int s = 0; s < numSamples; ++s)
            {
                bufL[s] *= busGain;
                bufR[s] *= busGain;
            }
        }

        // Route bus output to connected destinations (typically master)
        auto outConns = routing_->getOutputConnections(node->id);
        for (auto* conn : outConns)
        {
            if (!conn->active || conn->bypassed) continue;
            if (conn->type == ConnectionType::Sidechain) continue;

            auto itBuf = nodeBuffers_.find(conn->destNodeId);
            if (itBuf == nodeBuffers_.end()) continue;

            float routeGain = (conn->type == ConnectionType::Send ||
                               conn->type == ConnectionType::PreSend) ? conn->gain : 1.0f;
            auto& destBuf = itBuf->second;
            auto* dstL = destBuf.getWritePointer(0);
            auto* dstR = destBuf.getWritePointer(juce::jmin(1, destBuf.getNumChannels() - 1));
            for (int s = 0; s < numSamples; ++s)
            {
                dstL[s] += bufL[s] * routeGain;
                dstR[s] += bufR[s] * routeGain;
            }
        }
    }

    /** Process the Master node: copy summed mix to hardware output.
     *  All master processing (inserts â†’ FX chain â†’ fader â†’ meter â†’ render)
     *  is handled by MasterBusEngine in ApplicationCore::getNextAudioBlock(). */
    void processMasterNode(RoutingNode* node, int numSamples,
                           const juce::AudioSourceChannelInfo& output)
    {
        auto& masterBuf = nodeBuffers_[node->id];

        // Copy raw summed mix to hardware output â€” no plugin chain, no fader.
        // MasterBusEngine applies master inserts, FX chain, gain, mute,
        // metering, and render tap on this buffer next.
        auto* outL = output.buffer->getWritePointer(0, output.startSample);
        auto* outR = output.buffer->getWritePointer(
            juce::jmin(1, output.buffer->getNumChannels() - 1), output.startSample);
        auto* srcL = masterBuf.getReadPointer(0);
        auto* srcR = masterBuf.getReadPointer(juce::jmin(1, masterBuf.getNumChannels() - 1));

        for (int s = 0; s < numSamples; ++s)
        {
            outL[s] = srcL[s];
            outR[s] = srcR[s];
        }
    }

    // â”€â”€ Flat fallback (no routing graph) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
    void processFlat(const juce::AudioSourceChannelInfo& output,
                     int numSamples, bool isPlaying,
                     SamplePosition position, bool anySolo)
    {
        for (int i = 0; i < tracks_->getNumTracks(); ++i)
        {
            auto* track = tracks_->getTrack(i);
            if (!track) continue;

            bool audible = !track->isMuted();
            if (anySolo) audible = track->isSoloed();

            trackBuffer_.clear();

            if (clips_)
            {
                juce::ScopedTryLock cl(clips_->getLock());
                if (cl.isLocked())
                {
                    auto clipsOnTrack = clips_->getClipsOnTrack(track->getID());
                    for (auto* clip : clipsOnTrack)
                    {
                        if (clip->isMuted()) continue;
                        if (isPlaying) renderClip(clip, position, numSamples);
                    }
                }
            }

            if (pluginChains_)
            {
                auto it = pluginChains_->find(track->getID());
                if (it != pluginChains_->end() && it->second)
                    it->second->processBlock(trackBuffer_, numSamples);
            }

            float vol = track->getVolume();
            float pan = track->getPan();
            float leftGain  = vol * std::cos((pan + 1.f) * 0.25f * juce::MathConstants<float>::pi);
            float rightGain = vol * std::sin((pan + 1.f) * 0.25f * juce::MathConstants<float>::pi);

            float leftPeak  = trackBuffer_.getMagnitude(0, 0, numSamples) * leftGain;
            float rightPeak = trackBuffer_.getMagnitude(
                juce::jmin(1, trackBuffer_.getNumChannels() - 1), 0, numSamples) * rightGain;
            float displayLeft = juce::jmax(leftPeak, track->getPeakLevelLeft() * 0.92f);
            float displayRight = juce::jmax(rightPeak, track->getPeakLevelRight() * 0.92f);
            track->setPeakLevels(displayLeft, displayRight);

            if (audible)
            {
                auto* outL = output.buffer->getWritePointer(0, output.startSample);
                auto* outR = output.buffer->getWritePointer(
                    juce::jmin(1, output.buffer->getNumChannels() - 1), output.startSample);
                auto* srcL = trackBuffer_.getReadPointer(0);
                auto* srcR = trackBuffer_.getReadPointer(juce::jmin(1, trackBuffer_.getNumChannels() - 1));

                for (int s = 0; s < numSamples; ++s)
                {
                    outL[s] += srcL[s] * leftGain;
                    outR[s] += srcR[s] * rightGain;
                }
            }
        }

        // Master processing (inserts â†’ FX chain â†’ fader â†’ meter â†’ render)
        // is handled by MasterBusEngine in ApplicationCore::getNextAudioBlock().
        // No duplicate master processing here.
    }

    /** Render a single clip into trackBuffer_ at the current transport position. */
    void renderClip(Clip* clip, SamplePosition playPos, int numSamples)
    {
        auto clipStart = clip->getStartPosition();
        auto clipTimelineLength = clip->getLength();
        if (auto* ac = dynamic_cast<AudioClip*>(clip))
            clipTimelineLength = ac->getProcessedTimelineLength();
        auto clipEnd   = clipStart + clipTimelineLength;

        auto windowEnd = playPos + numSamples;
        if (playPos >= clipEnd || windowEnd <= clipStart) return;

        int bufferStart = (int)juce::jmax((SamplePosition)0, clipStart - playPos);
        int bufferEnd   = (int)juce::jmin((SamplePosition)numSamples, clipEnd - playPos);
        int count       = bufferEnd - bufferStart;
        if (count <= 0) return;

        SamplePosition engineClipOffset = (playPos + bufferStart) - clipStart;

        if (audioFiles_)
        {
            auto* srcBuf = audioFiles_->getBuffer(clip->getID());
            if (srcBuf && srcBuf->getNumSamples() > 0)
            {
                int srcTotal = srcBuf->getNumSamples();
                int srcChans = srcBuf->getNumChannels();
                double srcRate = audioFiles_->getSourceSampleRate(clip->getID());
                double srcPerEngineSample = srcRate / sampleRate_;
                float clipGain      = 1.0f;
                float clipPitch     = 0.0f;    // semitones (legacy)
                float clipStretch   = 1.0f;
                float fineTuneCents = 0.0f;
                float ultraDemonAmount = 0.0f;
                int   tpMode        = DAW::TimePitchModeIds::DefaultUserMode;
                int fadeInLength    = 0;
                int fadeOutLength   = 0;
                AudioClip* ac = dynamic_cast<AudioClip*>(clip);
                if (ac)
                {
                    clipGain      = ac->getGain();
                    clipPitch     = ac->getPitch();
                    clipStretch   = juce::jmax(0.01f, ac->getTimeStretch());
                    fineTuneCents = ac->getFineTuneCents();
                    ultraDemonAmount = ac->getUltraDemonAmount();
                    tpMode        = ac->getTimePitchMode();
                    fadeInLength  = (int) ac->getFadeInLength();
                    fadeOutLength = (int) ac->getFadeOutLength();

                    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
                    // FORENSIC AUDIT â€” Mode Value Trap
                    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
                    static int auditLogCounter = 0;
                    if (++auditLogCounter % 50 == 0)
                    {
                        DBG("[FORENSIC AUDIT renderClip] clipId=" << ac->getID());
                        DBG("  modeRaw=" << tpMode);
                        DBG("  pitchSemi=" << clipPitch);
                        DBG("  fineTune=" << fineTuneCents);
                        DBG("  stretch=" << clipStretch);
                        DBG("  gain=" << clipGain);
                    }
                }

                auto& clipPitchSmoother = getOrCreateClipPitchSmoother(clip->getID());
                clipPitchSmoother.setPitchTargetSemitones(clipPitch + fineTuneCents / 100.0f);
                const float smoothedClipPitch = clipPitchSmoother.getNextSemitones();
                for (int i = 1; i < count; ++i)
                    clipPitchSmoother.getNextSemitones();

                // â”€â”€ Pitch / time dispatch â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
                //
                // Mode 0 (Resample): pitch and time coupled â€” inline resample.
                //   Fast path, tape/DJ behavior, backward compatible.
                //
                // Mode 1-6: delegate to TimePitchDSPCore::renderClipSegment().
                //   AudioEngine does NOT implement stretch math here.
                //   All algorithm details live in the DSP nuclei.
                //   Each clip maintains its own TimePitchDSPCore instance
                //   (accessed via per-clip DSP map, or fallback inline for Mode 0).

                const double totalSt    = (double)smoothedClipPitch;
                const double pitchRatio = std::pow(2.0, totalSt / 12.0);

                DBG("[AUDIO RENDER] clip=" << clip->getID()
                    << " mode=" << tpMode
                    << " pitch=" << clipPitch
                    << " fineTune=" << fineTuneCents
                    << " stretch=" << clipStretch
                    << " AudioClip.getTimePitchMode()=" << (ac ? ac->getTimePitchMode() : -1)
                    << " AudioClip.getPitch()=" << (ac ? ac->getPitch() : 0.f)
                    << " AudioClip.getTimeStretch()=" << (ac ? ac->getTimeStretch() : 1.f));

                if (tpMode == DAW::TimePitchModeIds::Resample)
                {
                    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
                    // FORENSIC AUDIT â€” Resample/Tape Path Counter
                    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
                    ++PitchAuditCore::resampleTapeHits;

                    // â”€â”€ MODE 0: Resample (inline, fastest path) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
                    // pitch and duration change together (tape/vinyl/DJ)
                    const double readRate = pitchRatio / (double)clipStretch;

                    for (int ch = 0; ch < trackBuffer_.getNumChannels(); ++ch)
                    {
                        auto* dst = trackBuffer_.getWritePointer(ch, bufferStart);
                        int   sch = juce::jmin(ch, srcChans - 1);
                        auto* src = srcBuf->getReadPointer(sch);

                        for (int s = 0; s < count; ++s)
                        {
                            double srcPos = (double)clip->getSourceOffset()
                                          + ((double)(engineClipOffset + s) * readRate);
                            int si0 = (int)std::floor(srcPos);
                            int si1 = juce::jmin(si0 + 1, srcTotal - 1);
                            if (si0 < 0 || si0 >= srcTotal) continue;

                            float frac   = (float)(srcPos - (double)si0);
                            float sample = (src[si0] + (src[si1] - src[si0]) * frac) * clipGain;
                            int   posInClip = (int)(engineClipOffset + s);
                            float fadeGain = computeFadeGain(posInClip, (int)clipTimelineLength,
                                                             fadeInLength, fadeOutLength);
                            dst[s] += sample * fadeGain;
                        }
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
                    // â”€â”€ MODE 1-6: INDEPENDENT PITCH/TIME PROCESSING â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
                    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
                    //
                    // CRITICAL FIX: Detect if we need independent pitch shift.
                    // Independent pitch means:
                    //   - Pitch changes perceived frequency
                    //   - Clip duration stays EXACTLY the same
                    //   - Timeline length unchanged
                    //   - Output samples = Input samples ALWAYS
                    //
                    // This is different from Resample mode where:
                    //   readRate = pitchRatio / stretchRatio
                    //   causes duration change.
                    //
                    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•

                    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
                    // DETECTION: Which audio path should we use?
                    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•

                    const bool pitchActive =
                        std::abs(clipPitch) > 0.001f ||
                        std::abs(smoothedClipPitch) > 0.001f ||
                        std::abs(fineTuneCents) > 0.1f ||
                        ultraDemonAmount > 0.0001f;

                    const bool stretchActive =
                        std::abs(clipStretch - 1.0f) > 0.001f;

                    const bool independentPitchMode =
                        tpMode == TimePitchModeIds::PitchOnly ||
                        tpMode == TimePitchModeIds::Vocal;

                    const bool independentStretchMode =
                        tpMode == TimePitchModeIds::Stretch;

                    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
                    // ROUTER â€” Gate on which knob is active, NOT on mode alone.
                    // Precedence (order matters):
                    //   StretchThenPitch: both active in independent mode.
                    //     Step 1: stretch via DSP core (pitch=0) â†’ pitchInputBuffer_.
                    //     Step 2: independent pitch core on that buffer (Nâ†’N samples).
                    //     The pitch core's input-N â†’ output-N contract makes this safe.
                    //   StretchOnly: only stretch active (any mode) â†’ DSP core, falls
                    //     through to the existing dspCore block below with stretchOnlyPathHits.
                    //   PitchOnly: only pitch active in independent mode â†’ independentPitchCore_.
                    //   Normal: everything else â†’ DSP core with full state.
                    // Vocal/PitchOnly mode controls WHICH pitch algorithm; it does NOT
                    // override stretch-only audio onto the pitch path.
                    // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
                    enum class RenderPath { Normal, StretchOnly, PitchOnly, StretchThenPitch };
                    RenderPath renderPath;
                    if (pitchActive && stretchActive && independentPitchMode)
                        renderPath = RenderPath::StretchThenPitch;
                    else if (stretchActive && !pitchActive)
                        renderPath = RenderPath::StretchOnly;
                    else if (pitchActive && independentPitchMode)
                        renderPath = RenderPath::PitchOnly;
                    else
                        renderPath = RenderPath::Normal;

                    DBG("[ROUTER] path=" << (int)renderPath
                        << " pitchActive=" << (int)pitchActive
                        << " stretchActive=" << (int)stretchActive
                        << " mode=" << tpMode);

                    // useIndependentPitchPath: true for PitchOnly and StretchThenPitch
                    // (both need the independent pitch core in their final step).
                    const bool useIndependentPitchPath =
                        (renderPath == RenderPath::PitchOnly ||
                         renderPath == RenderPath::StretchThenPitch);

                    DBG("[PitchRoute] " << (useIndependentPitchPath
                        ? "independent path ACTIVE - legacy path BYPASSED"
                        : "legacy path ACTIVE"));

                    static int renderLogCounter = 0;
                    if (++renderLogCounter % 50 == 0) // log every 50 blocks
                    {
                        DBG("[AUDIO PATH DETECTION] mode=" << tpMode
                            << " pitch=" << clipPitch << "st"
                            << " fineTune=" << fineTuneCents << "ct"
                            << " stretch=" << clipStretch << "x");
                        DBG("[AUDIO PATH DETECTION] pitchActive=" << (pitchActive ? "true" : "false")
                            << " stretchActive=" << (stretchActive ? "true" : "false")
                            << " independentPitchMode=" << (independentPitchMode ? "true" : "false"));
                        DBG("[AUDIO PATH DECISION] useIndependentPitchPath=" << (useIndependentPitchPath ? "true" : "false"));
                        if (useIndependentPitchPath)
                            DBG("[AUDIO PATH] â†’ INDEPENDENT PITCH CORE (duration preserved)");
                        else
                            DBG("[AUDIO PATH] â†’ TimePitchDSPCore fallback (may couple pitch/time)");
                    }

                    if (useIndependentPitchPath)
                    {
                        // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
                        // FORENSIC AUDIT â€” Independent Pitch Path Counter
                        // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
                        ++PitchAuditCore::independentPitchPathHits;
                        // StretchThenPitch counts BOTH paths (stretch + pitch both run)
                        if (renderPath == RenderPath::StretchThenPitch)
                            ++PitchAuditCore::stretchOnlyPathHits;

                        // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
                        // âœ… INDEPENDENT PITCH PATH â€” HARD DISCONNECTED FROM STRETCH
                        // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
                        //
                        // ABSOLUTE RULES:
                        //   - NO readRate formula
                        //   - NO pitchRatio / stretchRatio coupling
                        //   - NO TimePitchDSPCore
                        //   - NO PhaseVocoderStretchCore
                        //   - Output samples = Input samples ALWAYS
                        //   - Clip duration NEVER changes
                        //
                        // If both pitch AND stretch are active:
                        //   â†’ First apply stretch (changes duration)
                        //   â†’ Then apply pitch (preserves duration)
                        //   â†’ Serial processing, no coupling formula
                        //
                        // PROOF: count variable never changes from input to output.
                        // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•

                        const int maxChans = juce::jmin(srcChans, pitchInputBuffer_.getNumChannels());

                        // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
                        // CASE 1: Stretch active â†’ use stretch first, then pitch
                        // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
                        if (renderPath == RenderPath::StretchThenPitch)
                        {
                            DBG("[INDEPENDENT PITCH] StretchThenPitch: Step 1 â€” stretch " << clipStretch << "x via DSP core");
                            // â”€â”€ Step 1: Stretch via DSP core (pitch=0) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
                            // Output written directly into pitchInputBuffer_ so the
                            // independent pitch core below reads pre-stretched audio.
                            // The pitch core's N-in / N-out contract preserves the
                            // stretched duration unchanged.
                            auto& dspCoreSTP = getOrCreateClipDSP(clip->getID());
                            {
                                const bool contSTP =
                                    (lastClipRenderBlock_.count(clip->getID()) &&
                                     lastClipRenderBlock_.at(clip->getID()) + 1 == processCounter_) &&
                                    (lastClipRenderEndOffset_.count(clip->getID()) &&
                                     lastClipRenderEndOffset_.at(clip->getID()) == engineClipOffset) &&
                                    (lastClipRenderMode_.count(clip->getID()) &&
                                     lastClipRenderMode_.at(clip->getID()) == tpMode);
                                if (!contSTP) dspCoreSTP.reset();
                            }
                            ArrangementEditor::TimePitchState tsSTP;
                            tsSTP.pitchSemitones   = 0.0;  // pitch=0: stretch only in step 1
                            tsSTP.fineTuneCents    = 0.0;
                            tsSTP.stretchRatio     = clipStretch;
                            tsSTP.mode             = ArrangementEditor::TimePitchMode::Stretch;
                            tsSTP.preserveFormants = false;
                            tsSTP.formantSemitones = 0.0;
                            if (ac)
                            {
                                dspCoreSTP.setState(tsSTP);
                            }
                            const auto srcStartSTP = ac
                                ? juce::jmax<int64_t>((int64_t)clip->getSourceOffset(), ac->getSourceStartSample())
                                : (int64_t)clip->getSourceOffset();
                            const auto srcEndSTP = ac
                                ? (ac->getSourceEndSample() > 0
                                   ? juce::jmin<int64_t>(ac->getSourceEndSample(), (int64_t)srcTotal)
                                   : (int64_t)srcTotal)
                                : (int64_t)srcTotal;
                            for (int ch = 0; ch < maxChans; ++ch)
                            {
                                ArrangementEditor::TimePitchRenderRequest reqSTP;
                                reqSTP.sourceData         = srcBuf->getReadPointer(juce::jmin(ch, srcChans - 1));
                                reqSTP.sourceTotalSamples = srcTotal;
                                reqSTP.sourceStartSample  = srcStartSTP;
                                reqSTP.sourceEndSample    = srcEndSTP;
                                reqSTP.outputData         = pitchInputBuffer_.getWritePointer(ch);
                                reqSTP.numOutputSamples   = count;
                                reqSTP.channel            = ch;
                                reqSTP.state              = tsSTP;
                                reqSTP.outputSampleRate   = sampleRate_;
                                reqSTP.callerTag          = "STRETCH_THEN_PITCH";
                                dspCoreSTP.renderClipSegment(reqSTP);
                            }
                            DBG("[INDEPENDENT PITCH] StretchThenPitch: Step 2 â€” pitch " << smoothedClipPitch << "st via independent core");
                            // pitchInputBuffer_ is now filled with stretched audio.
                            // The pitch core block below reads from it directly.
                        }

                        // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
                        // CASE 2: Pitch active (with or without stretch)
                        // Read source at NORMAL SPEED and apply pitch shift
                        // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

                        if (pitchActive)
                        {
                            DBG("[INDEPENDENT PITCH] Pitch active: raw=" << clipPitch << "st smoothed=" << smoothedClipPitch << "st");
                            DBG("[INDEPENDENT PITCH] Reading " << count << " samples at NORMAL SPEED (no readRate)");
                        }

                        // Step 1: Read source at normal speed into pitch input buffer.
                        // PROOF: Reading exactly count samples, no rate modification.
                        // Skip for StretchThenPitch â€” pitchInputBuffer_ was already filled
                        // by the dspCore stretch pass above.
                        if (renderPath != RenderPath::StretchThenPitch)
                        for (int ch = 0; ch < maxChans; ++ch)
                        {
                            auto* src = srcBuf->getReadPointer(juce::jmin(ch, srcChans - 1));
                            auto* dst = pitchInputBuffer_.getWritePointer(ch);

                            for (int s = 0; s < count; ++s)
                            {
                                const int64_t srcIdx = clip->getSourceOffset() + engineClipOffset + s;
                                dst[s] = (srcIdx >= 0 && srcIdx < srcTotal) ? src[(int)srcIdx] : 0.f;
                            }
                        }

                        if (pitchActive)
                            DBG("[INDEPENDENT PITCH] Read complete: " << count << " samples in pitch input buffer");

                        // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
                        // Step 2: Independent pitch shift (PRESERVES sample count)
                        // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

                        if (pitchActive)
                        {
                            ArrangementEditor::DemonVoiceRenderParams demonParams;
                            demonParams.demonAmount = ultraDemonAmount;
                            demonParams.preset = ArrangementEditor::DemonVoicePreset::UltraDemon;
                            demonParams.basePitchSemitones = smoothedClipPitch;
                            demonParams.fineTuneCents = 0.0f;
                            demonParams.manualFormantSemitones = ac ? ac->getFormantSemitones() : 0.f;
                            demonParams.channels = maxChans;
                            demonParams.sampleRate = (int) sampleRate_;

                            DBG("[INDEPENDENT PITCH] Calling DemonVoiceEngineCore.process()");
                            DBG("[INDEPENDENT PITCH]   Input samples:  " << count);
                            DBG("[INDEPENDENT PITCH]   Demon amount: " << ultraDemonAmount);
                            DBG("[INDEPENDENT PITCH]   Expected output: " << count << " samples (SAME)");

                            const float* inPtrs[2] = {
                                pitchInputBuffer_.getReadPointer(0),
                                pitchInputBuffer_.getReadPointer(juce::jmin(1, maxChans - 1))
                            };
                            float* outPtrs[2] = {
                                pitchOutputBuffer_.getWritePointer(0),
                                pitchOutputBuffer_.getWritePointer(juce::jmin(1, pitchOutputBuffer_.getNumChannels() - 1))
                            };

                            if (ultraDemonAmount > 0.0001f)
                            {
                                const auto targets = demonVoiceEngine_.process(inPtrs, outPtrs, maxChans, count, demonParams);
                                DBG("[DEMON VOICE] pitch=" << targets.pitchSemitones
                                    << " formant=" << targets.formantSemitones
                                    << " drive=" << targets.drivePercent
                                    << " sub=" << targets.subPercent);
                            }
                            else
                            {
                                ArrangementEditor::ClipPitchProcessParams pitchParams;
                                pitchParams.pitchSemitones = smoothedClipPitch;
                                pitchParams.fineTuneCents = 0.0;
                                pitchParams.formantSemitones = ac ? ac->getFormantSemitones() : 0.0;
                                pitchParams.preserveFormants = ac ? ac->getPreserveFormants() : false;
                                pitchParams.sampleRate = (int) sampleRate_;
                                pitchParams.channels = maxChans;
                                auto& pitchCore = getOrCreateClipPitchCore(clip->getID());
                                pitchCore.processBlock(inPtrs, outPtrs, maxChans, count, pitchParams);
                            }

                            DBG("[INDEPENDENT PITCH] âœ… PROOF: processBlock received " << count
                                << " samples in, produced " << count << " samples out.");
                            DBG("[INDEPENDENT PITCH] âœ… Duration is UNCHANGED.");
                            DBG("[INDEPENDENT PITCH] âœ… NO coupling with stretch.");
                            DBG("[INDEPENDENT PITCH] âœ… NO readRate formula used.");
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            // Identity: pitch = 0, just copy input to output
                            DBG("[INDEPENDENT PITCH] Identity path: pitch = 0, copying " << count << " samples");
                            for (int ch = 0; ch < maxChans; ++ch)
                            {
                                const float* in = pitchInputBuffer_.getReadPointer(ch);
                                float* out = pitchOutputBuffer_.getWritePointer(juce::jmin(ch, pitchOutputBuffer_.getNumChannels() - 1));
                                std::memcpy(out, in, sizeof(float) * (size_t)count);
                            }
                        }

                        // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
                        // Step 3: Demon-voice post-processing (dry when pitch is musical)
                        //   a) Tape resample blend  â€” physical 'bigger throat' character
                        //   b) FormantShiftCore     â€” decouple formant from pitch
                        //   c) DarkVoiceBodyCore    â€” low-mid lift + high-shelf cut
                        // All three are bypassed (near-zero cost) in the musical range.
                        // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
                        if (pitchActive)
                        {
                            ArrangementEditor::TimePitchState demonTs;
                            const double effectivePitchSemitones = ultraDemonAmount > 0.0001f
                                ? (double)smoothedClipPitch
                                : getOrCreateClipPitchCore(clip->getID()).getCurrentPitchSemitones();
                            demonTs.pitchSemitones = effectivePitchSemitones;
                            demonTs.fineTuneCents  = 0.0;
                            demonTs.stretchRatio   = 1.0;
                            demonTs.mode           = ArrangementEditor::TimePitchMode::Vocal;
                            const ArrangementEditor::UnifiedPitchSnapshot demonSnap =
                                ArrangementEditor::UnifiedPitchStateCore::snapshotFromState(demonTs);

                            // â”€â”€ a) Tape resample blend â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
                            if (demonSnap.tapeBlend > 0.0001)
                            {
                                auto& tapeCore = getOrCreateTapeCore(clip->getID());
                                tapeCore.setPitchScale(demonSnap.pitchScale);
                                tapeCore.setWailAmount(0.0); // wail is out-of-scope for this build

                                for (int ch = 0; ch < maxChans; ++ch)
                                {
                                    if (tapeScratchBuf_.getNumSamples() < count)
                                        tapeScratchBuf_.setSize(2, count, false, true, true);

                                    const float* srcPtr = srcBuf->getReadPointer(juce::jmin(ch, srcChans - 1));
                                    tapeCore.renderSegment(srcPtr, srcTotal,
                                                           (int64_t)clip->getSourceOffset() + (int64_t)engineClipOffset,
                                                           (int64_t)srcTotal,
                                                           tapeScratchBuf_.getWritePointer(ch),
                                                           count, ch);

                                    // Linear crossfade: grain â†’ tape.
                                    // Equal-power (sqrt) was wrong here â€” it summed two
                                    // pitch-shifted voices at intermediate blend values,
                                    // producing the audible double-voice. Linear blend
                                    // ensures exactly one voice is heard at any point.
                                    const float tBlend = 0.0f;
                                    const float iBlend = 1.0f - tBlend;
                                    float* out        = pitchOutputBuffer_.getWritePointer(ch);
                                    const float* tape = tapeScratchBuf_.getReadPointer(ch);
                                    for (int s = 0; s < count; ++s)
                                        out[s] = out[s] * iBlend + tape[s] * tBlend;
                                }
                            }

                            // â”€â”€ b) Formant shift â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
                            if (std::abs(demonSnap.formantScale - 1.0) > 0.01)
                            {
                                auto& formantCore = getOrCreateFormantCore(clip->getID());
                                for (int ch = 0; ch < maxChans; ++ch)
                                    formantCore.process(pitchOutputBuffer_.getWritePointer(ch),
                                                        count, ch, demonSnap);
                            }

                            // â”€â”€ c) Dark body EQ â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
                            if (demonSnap.darkIntensity >= 0.05)
                            {
                                auto& bodyCore = getOrCreateBodyCore(clip->getID());
                                for (int ch = 0; ch < maxChans; ++ch)
                                    bodyCore.process(pitchOutputBuffer_.getWritePointer(ch),
                                                     count, ch, demonSnap);
                            }
                        }

                        // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
                        // Step 4: Apply gain/fades and mix to track buffer
                        // PROOF: Still mixing exactly count samples
                        // â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

                        DBG("[PitchRoute] output write count = 1");
                        DBG("[INDEPENDENT PITCH] Mixing " << count << " samples to track buffer");

                        for (int ch = 0; ch < trackBuffer_.getNumChannels(); ++ch)
                        {
                            auto* trackDst = trackBuffer_.getWritePointer(ch, bufferStart);
                            const auto* pitchSrc = pitchOutputBuffer_.getReadPointer(juce::jmin(ch, maxChans - 1));

                            for (int s = 0; s < count; ++s)
                            {
                                float sample = pitchSrc[s] * clipGain;
                                int posInClip = (int)(engineClipOffset + s);
                                float fadeGain = computeFadeGain(posInClip, (int)clipTimelineLength,
                                                                 fadeInLength, fadeOutLength);
                                trackDst[s] += sample * fadeGain;
                            }
                        }

                        DBG("[INDEPENDENT PITCH] âœ…âœ…âœ… COMPLETE âœ…âœ…âœ…");
                        DBG("[INDEPENDENT PITCH] Input:  " << count << " samples");
                        DBG("[INDEPENDENT PITCH] Output: " << count << " samples");
